Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any symmetric module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 939. The projective resolution has length at most 245 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 939 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the symmetric structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 245. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
